This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   I am the Research Reviewer (Loop #2) for the `geosafe_vla` project targeting CoRL 2026 (Abstract: 5/26, Paper: 5/29). My role is independent scientific verification - read-only access to registry/SSH/wandb/logs, output only via `reviewer_post_to_director`. Primary tasks this session:
   - Continuous monitoring of D005 training (baseline_v3_s1 + geosafe_v3_s1, 30K steps each)
   - Reviewing worker outputs for correctness (GPU compliance, code changes, paper edits)
   - Director-requested strategic assessments: (a) narrative strength evaluation, (b) full paper read-through review, (c) CoRL simulated peer review
   - Proactive identification of issues (ETA miscalculation, SafeLIBERO task count error, missing related works)

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **GeoSafe method**: SDF auxiliary head on π₀.₅ VLA, collision-shaping loss with noise-aware w(t) gating, head discarded at inference for zero overhead
   - **Flow-matching VLA**: π₀.₅ generates action trajectories via velocity field v_θ(x_t, o, t), noise interpolant x_t = (1-t)a + tε
   - **w(t) = σ(α(t₀-t))**: Sigmoid gating with t₀=0.5, α=10, suppresses collision penalty at high noise
   - **Loss**: L_total = L_FM + λ_SDF·L_SDF + λ_col·L_col (λ_SDF=0.1, λ_col=0.01, δ=2cm)
   - **SafeLIBERO benchmark**: 4 suites (spatial=4, object=4, goal=5, long=4) × 2 levels = 34 scenarios
   - **Metrics**: TSR (task success rate), CAR (collision avoidance rate), SS = TSR × CAR
   - **DDP training**: 2 GPUs per run, bs=128 effective, 30K steps, warmup 10K, cosine decay, bf16
   - **Ablations**: A1 (λ_col=0, no collision loss), A2 (no backbone gradient), A3 (constant w(t)=1 via t₀=100)
   - **GPU allocation**: baseline GPU 0,1 port 29500; geosafe GPU 2,3 port 29501; A1+A2 parallel, A3 serial after A1

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - **`/root/geosafe_vla/paper/main.tex`** (284 lines)
     - Full paper with 13+ placeholders awaiting D005 data
     - Key fixes verified: 32→34 scenarios (4 occurrences), Goal 4→5 tasks, "may require"→"require", deployment complexity paragraph added
     - Title: "GeoSafe: Internalizing Collision Geometry into Flow-Matching VLA Weights for Zero-Overhead Safe Manipulation"
     - 3 contributions: C1 (SDF internalization), C2 (w(t) gating), C3 (empirical improvement)
   
   - **`/root/geosafe_vla/paper/appendix.tex`** (375 lines)
     - Implementation details, training config tables, SDF label computation, SafeLIBERO details
     - Extended ablation tables (noise-aware weight variants, loss weight sensitivity, threshold sensitivity, SDF coverage impact) - all placeholder
     - No leftover "32 scenarios" - only "32" is D_a=32 action dimension
   
   - **`/root/geosafe_vla/paper/abstract_corl_draft.txt`**
     - Standalone abstract for CoRL submission, uses conservative language without specific numbers
     - "demonstrates improved collision avoidance while maintaining competitive task success"
     - "Ablation studies isolate each component's contribution"
   
   - **`/root/geosafe_vla/paper/references.bib`** (18 entries)
     - VLA: pi0, pi0.5; Safety: AEGIS, SafeVLA, OmniGuide, SafeDiffuser; Geometry VLA: GLaD, OccVLA, Spatial Forcing, GeoPredict; SDF: CSSDF-Net, NGDF, DeepSDF
     - Missing: Safety-Guided Flow (2603.13300), CoFreeVLA (2601.21712), GeoAware-VLA (2509.14117), VLA Safety Survey (2604.23775)
   
   - **`/root/geosafe_vla/scripts/train_ablation_a1.sh`**
     - CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0,1, port 29500, sdf_collision_weight=0.0, sdf_loss_weight=0.1, --sdf_grad_to_backbone
   
   - **`/root/geosafe_vla/scripts/train_ablation_a2.sh`**
     - CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=2,3, port 29501, sdf_collision_weight=0.01, NO --sdf_grad_to_backbone
   
   - **`/root/geosafe_vla/scripts/train_ablation_a3.sh`**
     - CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0,1, port 29500, sdf_noise_t0=100 (constant w(t)=1), --sdf_grad_to_backbone
   
   - **`/root/geosafe_vla/scripts/eval_learning_curve.sh`**
     - Evaluates 6 checkpoints (5K-30K), 10 tasks × 20 episodes, server-client architecture, GPU_SERVE=0 GPU_EVAL=1
   
   - **`/root/geosafe_vla/scripts/eval_safelibero_batch.sh`**
     - 4 SafeLIBERO suites, 17 tasks total, server-client with per-step JSON output, parses TSR/CAR/SS
   
   - **`/root/geosafe_vla/eval_baseline.py`**
     - SafeLIBERO eval script, tracks TSR and CAR, imports from vlsa-aegis/safelibero
     - Has --task-suite-name, --video-out-path, --num-trials-per-task args
   
   - **`/root/geosafe_vla/vlsa-aegis/safelibero/libero/libero/benchmark/libero_suite_task_map.py`**
     - Ground truth task counts: spatial=4, object=4, goal=5 (5 tasks listed), long=4
   
   - **Registry** (`registry.yaml`): 10 experiments total, 2 RUNNING (baseline_v3_s1, geosafe_v3_s1), 5 DONE success, 3 DONE failed/killed
   
   - **ROADMAP.md**: CoRL 2026 targeting, D005 30K training, ablation schedule, GPU budget 468 vs 816 GPU-h available

4. Errors and fixes:
   - **ssh_read_only pipe metacharacter block**: Commands with `|` are blocked. Fixed by running separate grep commands for each pattern instead of piped commands.
   - **Paper-quality-lessons skill path**: Initially tried relative path from session directory, file not found. Fixed by using absolute path `/home/ubuntu/agent-ml-research/skills/global/paper-quality-lessons.skill.md`.
   - **grep `---` pattern error**: `grep -nc "---"` was interpreted as a flag. Not critical, worked around.
   - **Worker ETA overestimate**: Worker progress-check-3 reported D005 completion ETA ~May 23 07:00-08:00 UTC, ~8h later than actual rate-based calculation (~May 22 23:00-24:00 UTC). Posted correction to Director.
   - **SafeLIBERO task count error in paper**: Paper said "32 collision scenarios" and "Goal (4 tasks)" but code shows Goal has 5 tasks = 34 scenarios. Flagged to Director, worker fixed all 4 occurrences in main.tex.

5. Problem Solving:
   - **GPU compliance verification**: Independently verified CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES from /proc/{PID}/environ for all 4 training processes. All within project GPU whitelist (0-3).
   - **Training progress tracking**: collision_rate for geosafe_v3_s1 went from 0.628 (step 4724) to 0.632 (step 8350) - essentially flat during warmup, correcting the previous handoff's "slowly declining" characterization. Flagged as info-level.
   - **Paper consistency audit**: Found and flagged: "may require" vs "require" inconsistency (fixed), SafeLIBERO count error (fixed), Related Work missing deployment complexity mention (fixed).
   - **Related work gap identification**: Web searched and found 4 missing citations (SGF, CoFreeVLA, GeoAware-VLA, VLA Safety Survey) that CoRL reviewers would likely notice.

6. All user messages:
   - **[HANDOFF] Loop #2 start**: Provided detailed task_context with hard constraints, infrastructure snapshot, current state, pending tasks, strategic context. Required: (1) read_registry to confirm state, (2) reply confirming constraints + active experiments.
   - **[worker_done: worker_ops_d005-progress-check-2]**: Worker completed D005 progress check, asked to review.

   - **[Director 请求审查] 论文叙事线强度评估**: Director requested narrative strength assessment during training wait. Asked about: (1) claim strength given current evidence, (2) framing risk assessment for zero-overhead, collision_rate trends, AEGIS comparison. Requested claim strength rating, biggest risks, framing adjustment suggestions.
   - **[worker_done: worker_paper_results-non-data-content]**: Paper Results non-data content worker completed.

   - **[worker_done: worker_paper_zero-overhead-framing]**: Zero-overhead framing worker completed.

   - **[worker_done: worker_ops_read-abstract-draft]**: Abstract draft reader worker completed.
   - **[worker_done: worker_ops_abstract-wording-fix]**: Abstract wording fix worker completed.
   - **[Director 请求审查] CoRL 模拟审稿**: Director requested full simulated CoRL peer review. Asked for: Strengths (3-5), Weaknesses (3-5), Questions for Authors, Missing Experiments, Related Work Gaps, Overall Score (1-10). Specified to assume optimistic scenario (CAR improvement + TSR gap ≤5pp).
   - Multiple "Continue from where you left off" messages (system continuations when no response was needed).

7. Pending Tasks:
   - **Monitor D005 training to 10K+ steps**: Warmup ends at 10K, need to check if collision_rate starts declining post-warmup. Scheduled wakeup was set for ~1h but may need re-scheduling.
   - **D005 30K completion review**: When training finishes (~5/22 23:00 UTC), review eval results (TSR/CAR/SS), cross-verify with registry and logs.
   - **Ablation A1+A2 launch review**: When started, verify GPU assignments (A1=0,1 A2=2,3), port no conflict, D005 processes exited.

8. Current Work:
